module QtWebSockets
	WebSocket QQmlWebSocket
	WebSocketServer QQmlWebSocketServer
	type QQmlWebSocket WebSocket
		prototype QObject
		enum Status Connecting Open Closing Closed Error
		prop url QUrl
		prop requestedSubprotocols QString list
		prop status Status readonly
		prop errorString QString readonly
		prop active bool
		prop negotiatedSubprotocol QString readonly
		signal textMessageReceived message
		signal binaryMessageReceived message
		signal statusChanged status
		signal activeChanged isActive
		signal errorStringChanged errorString
		signal urlChanged
		signal requestedSubprotocolsChanged
		signal negotiatedSubprotocolChanged
		signal pong elapsedTime payload
		method onError
		method onStateChanged
		method sendTextMessage
		method sendBinaryMessage
		method ping
	type QQmlWebSocketServer WebSocketServer
		prototype QObject
		prop url QUrl readonly
		prop host QString
		prop port int
		prop name QString
		prop supportedSubprotocols QString list
		prop errorString QString readonly
		prop listen bool
		prop accept bool
		signal clientConnected webSocket
		signal errorStringChanged errorString
		signal urlChanged url
		signal portChanged port
		signal nameChanged name
		signal supportedSubprotocolsChanged supportedProtocols
		signal hostChanged host
		signal listenChanged listen
		signal acceptChanged accept
